It's... it's gotten bad enough that people at the office are starting to notice I'm falling behind. So... yeah, just really slowed down.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite or weight lately?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess. I don't really have much of an appetite. I'm not trying to lose weight or anything, it's just... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Have you had periods like this before, where you felt down and lost interest in things, or is this the first time you've felt this way?\n\nPatient: Um... no.
